# REVIEW-061 leg A — diagrams on seven public pages, checked against the claims register

You are a documentation and code reviewer for an open-source research project,
checking new diagrams on a public demonstration site for accuracy before they are
published. The project studies what XLS-96 (confidential Multi-Purpose Tokens on
the XRP Ledger) hides and what it does not, and keeps a claims register,
`CLAIMS.md`, that governs every sentence it publishes: **E** rows are established
evidence, **F** findings, **P** standing prohibitions, **B** blockers, **R**
corrections. Where a page and a row disagree, the row wins. Agreement is not
useful to the project; a label that holds is worth reporting only if you checked
it and say against what.

The owner ruled two reads before deployment: this one, against the register and
the repository, and a separate newcomer read that sees only screenshots. No later
round is planned, so report everything now.

## STEP 0 — do this first and print the results

1. Run `pwd` and `git log --oneline`. The working copy holds exactly two commits:
   the project tree before the diagrams and after them. **`git diff HEAD~1 HEAD`
   is the subject.** Work only inside this directory.
2. `review-input/rebuilt/` is the site built from the second tree by the
   project's release build, gated (70 of 70 controls) and checked in a headless
   browser (7 pages, all requests same-origin). `review-input/screens/` holds
   screenshots of that build, desktop at 1100 pixels and phone at 390, cut into
   segments. `review-input/rebuilt-pages-visible-text.md` is the seven pages'
   visible text. All three are untracked.
3. **Do not read** anything outside this directory, in particular any path under
   `~/.claude`, `~/.codex`, `~/.wconf`, `/tmp/wconf`, `~/Library`, any
   `scratchpad` directory, and anything under `/Users/*/Development`. They hold
   the owner's private configuration for a test network and you do not need
   them. Do not run `make check-web-hosted` or `tools/check-no-secrets.ts`: both
   need that private store. `runChecks()` in `tools/check-web-hosted.ts` is
   exported and needs no store.
4. You may write scratch files under `$TMPDIR` or a `review-work/` directory
   here, and run `bun install --frozen-lockfile` and `bun test`. Do not commit,
   push, or edit the two committed trees except in copies.

## What changed

1. `web/app/src/hosted/Diagrams.tsx`, new: nine diagrams drawn in markup (the
   site's policy refuses inline styles), and a colour key: amber means public,
   green hidden from the public, violet that the issuer can read it.
2. The diagrams placed on the pages: the overview (`routes/index.tsx`): where
   the line falls between public and hidden, and how the site is made; the
   explainer (`routes/how-it-works.tsx`): a position's journey with the inbox,
   which replaced three cards, who can read what, the facility, and a new panel
   on the project's architecture; `/observer` and `/issuer`: one position, field
   by field; `/capabilities` and `/wallet`: the two keys; `/custody`: the page's
   two findings.
3. `web/hosted/browser-check.ts`: the self-test now chooses the script chunk
   every page loads from the pages themselves, instead of a chunk by file name;
   the old name stopped existing when the diagrams module was added.

## Section 1 — every diagram label, arrow, colour and caption

For each diagram: does each box, edge label and caption say what its page and
the rows the page cites say? Report a label that says **more** (more hidden than
is hidden, more leaked than leaks, a power or property not established), says
**less** in a way that changes the meaning, or is supported by nothing. Then
the parts that are not words: does an **arrow** connect the two things the
source connects, in the right direction? Does a **grouping** imply something
false? Does each box's **colour** match the key's meaning for what it shows? The
architecture panel must match `README.md`'s *How it fits together*; the facility
diagram `docs/explainer/FACILITY-WALKTHROUGH-2026-08-20.md` and E-146, B-18,
F-24, F-26; the position diagram the observer page's lists in
`web/api/observer.ts`; the key diagrams the capability rows in
`web/api/capability.ts`; the custody diagram `web/app/src/views/CustodyView.tsx`.

## Section 2 — consistency

Between the diagrams (the three visible-zero cases, who can read, the inbox),
between each diagram and the text on its own page, and with the README. A label
true in one diagram and contradicted or narrowed in another is a finding.

## Section 3 — the code

`Diagrams.tsx`: any inline style, any ledger value, any wording the gate's
no-issuer-figure check (8b) would read as a figure, any markup that breaks the
pages' structure or accessibility (the diagrams are figures with captions). The
self-test change: can it choose a chunk that does not carry its controls' effect
to the pages each control inspects, choose differently between runs, or pass when
a control is not exercised? Test on copies of `review-input/rebuilt/` where you
can.

## Section 4 — reading the layout

If you can view images, read `review-input/screens/`: does any diagram read
wrongly at either width, in a way the markup alone would not show? If you cannot
view images, say so and skip this section.

## Output

Write your full report to `review-work/report.md`, then print the whole of it to
stdout, in this order:
1. STEP 0's results.
2. Findings, most severe first. For each: severity (HIGH: a published label is
   false or claims more than the evidence; MEDIUM: misleading, or missing a
   condition that changes the meaning; LOW: imprecise), the file and line, the
   label quoted exactly, the source you checked it against, and the smallest
   change that would fix it.
3. For each section, an explicit *"no findings"* if you found none, with what
   you checked.
4. What you did not check, and why.
End with the line `END OF LEG A REPORT`.

+/**
+ * The hosted demo's diagrams, at the owner's request of 2026-10-22.
+ *
+ * ⚠ **Markup, not images.** The policy's `style-src 'self'` refuses inline
+ * styles, and a diagram drawn in markup reflows on a phone, follows dark mode,
+ * and is text the gate reads like any other sentence. So every label is a
+ * claim: each restates a sentence already on the page it sits on, or a row the
+ * page cites. The words *decrypt* and *plaintext* are not used, for check 8b's
+ * reason (see `routes/how-it-works.tsx`).
+ *
+ * ⚠ **No ledger value.** Only `/observer` and `/issuer` may render one (check
+ * 8), and these diagrams render none on any page.
+ */
+import type { ReactNode } from "react";
+
+type Tone = "public" | "hidden" | "issuer" | "plain";
+
+const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
+  public: "border-amber-400 bg-amber-50 text-amber-950 dark:border-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-100",
+  hidden: "border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-950 dark:border-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-100",
+  issuer: "border-violet-400 bg-violet-50 text-violet-950 dark:border-violet-500 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-100",
+  plain: "border-black/20 dark:border-white/25",
+};
+
+const TONE_WORD: Record<Exclude<Tone, "plain">, string> = {
+  public: "public",
+  hidden: "hidden from the public",
+  issuer: "the issuer can read it",
+};
+
+function Figure({ caption, children }: { caption: string; children: ReactNode }) {
+  return (
+    <figure className="my-4">
+      {children}
+      <figcaption className="text-xs opacity-70 mt-2">{caption}</figcaption>
+    </figure>
+  );
+}
+
+/** A box. `sub` is a short description; `field` is a ledger field name, set in code. */
+function Node({ tone = "plain", title, sub, field, children, dashed = false }: {
+  tone?: Tone; title: string; sub?: string; field?: string; children?: ReactNode; dashed?: boolean;
+}) {
+  return (
+    <div className={`rounded-md border ${dashed ? "border-dashed" : ""} ${TONE[tone]} p-2 text-xs leading-snug`}>
+      <div className="font-semibold">{title}</div>
+      {field && <code className="block text-[10px] opacity-70 break-all mt-0.5">{field}</code>}
+      {sub && <div className="text-[11px] opacity-80 mt-0.5">{sub}</div>}
+      {children && <div className="mt-1">{children}</div>}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/** An arrow with its label: across on a wide screen, down on a phone. */
+function Edge({ label, tone, always }: { label: string; tone?: Tone; always?: "down" }) {
+  const arrow = always === "down"
+    ? <span aria-hidden="true">↓</span>
+    : <><span aria-hidden="true" className="hidden sm:inline">→</span><span aria-hidden="true" className="sm:hidden">↓</span></>;
+  return (
+    <div className="flex sm:flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-center text-[11px] leading-tight px-1">
+      <span className="text-base opacity-70">{arrow}</span>
+      <span className={tone && tone !== "plain" ? `rounded border px-1 ${TONE[tone]}` : "opacity-80"}>{label}</span>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function Down({ label, tone }: { label: string; tone?: Tone }) {
+  return <Edge label={label} tone={tone} always="down" />;
+}
+
+export function Legend({ tones = ["public", "hidden", "issuer"] }: { tones?: Exclude<Tone, "plain">[] }) {
+  return (
+    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-[11px] mt-1" aria-label="Key to the colours">
+      {tones.map((t) => (
+        <span key={t} className={`rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 ${TONE[t]}`}>{TONE_WORD[t]}</span>
+      ))}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/** Overview: where the line between public and hidden falls. */
+export function PerimeterDiagram() {
+  return (
+    <Figure caption="Amounts are public where they cross into or out of confidential form, and hidden inside it. Who transacts with whom is public everywhere, and the issuer reads every balance.">
+      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_2fr_auto_1fr] items-stretch">
+        <Node tone="public" title="A public balance" sub="an ordinary MPT holding" />
+        <Edge label="Convert: amount public" tone="public" />
+        <Node tone="hidden" title="Confidential balances">
+          <ul className="list-disc pl-4 space-y-0.5">
+            <li>each holder&rsquo;s balance, unless it is a recognisable zero: a new balance, an inbox just merged, or a position clawed back</li>
+            <li>the amount of each Send between holders</li>
+          </ul>
+        </Node>
+        <Edge label="ConvertBack: amount public" tone="public" />
+        <Node tone="public" title="A public balance" sub="an ordinary MPT holding" />
+      </div>
+      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 mt-2">
+        <Node tone="public" title="Public everywhere">
+          Who sends to whom, when and how often; the total held in confidential form.
+        </Node>
+        <Node tone="issuer" title="The issuer reads every confidential balance">
+          So does an auditor, if the token names one. No setting turns this off.
+        </Node>
+      </div>
+      <Legend />
+    </Figure>
+  );
+}
+
+/** Overview: how this site is made, and where signing happens instead. */
+export function ThisSiteDiagram() {
+  return (
+    <Figure caption="This site is files built once from a Devnet snapshot. Nothing on it can sign; signing happens only in a separate program on a holder's own machine.">
+      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1fr_auto_1fr_auto_1fr] items-stretch">
+        <Node title="Devnet, a public node" sub="read once, when the site is built" />
+        <Edge label="one snapshot" />
+        <Node title="The build" sub="pages built offline, checked by the release gate" />
+        <Edge label="static files" />
+        <Node title="Vercel" sub="serves the files; runs none of this project's code" />
+        <Edge label="your page load" />
+        <Node title="Your browser" sub="requests only this site's own files" />
+      </div>
+      <div className="mt-2">
+        <Node dashed title="Not part of this site: wconf-signer, on the holder's own machine">
+          Holds the keys, builds the proofs and signs. A web page cannot reach it.
+        </Node>
+      </div>
+    </Figure>
+  );
+}
+
+/** How it works: one position's journey, with the inbox. */
+export function LifecycleDiagram() {
+  return (
+    <Figure caption="Holder A converts, merges and sends to holder B, who merges and converts back. Only the Send hides its amount.">
+      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_13rem_1fr] items-center">
+        <div className="space-y-1">
+          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold opacity-80">Holder A</div>
+          <Node tone="public" title="Public balance" />
+          <Down label="Convert: amount public" tone="public" />
+          <Node tone="hidden" title="Inbox" field="ConfidentialBalanceInbox" />
+          <Down label="MergeInbox" />
+          <Node tone="hidden" title="Spending balance" field="ConfidentialBalanceSpending" />
+        </div>
+        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
+          <Edge label="Send: amount hidden; A and B public" tone="hidden" />
+          <Node title="The Send carries">
+            the amount encrypted for the sender, the receiver, the issuer and any auditor, and proofs every validator checks
+          </Node>
+        </div>
+        <div className="space-y-1">
+          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold opacity-80">Holder B</div>
+          <Node tone="hidden" title="Inbox" field="ConfidentialBalanceInbox" />
+          <Down label="MergeInbox" />
+          <Node tone="hidden" title="Spending balance" field="ConfidentialBalanceSpending" />
+          <Down label="ConvertBack: amount public" tone="public" />
+          <Node tone="public" title="Public balance" />
+        </div>
+      </div>
+      <Legend tones={["public", "hidden"]} />
+    </Figure>
+  );
+}

+// ⚠ Neutral on purpose: elsewhere green means "hidden from the public" and amber
+// "public", so a green "yes" here would say the opposite of what it means.
+const YES = "border-black/25 dark:border-white/30 font-semibold";
+const NO = "border-black/10 dark:border-white/15 opacity-60";
+const PART = "border-black/25 dark:border-white/30 border-dashed";
+
+/** How it works: who can read what. The walkthrough's §4 table, made general. */
+export function WhoSeesWhatDiagram() {
+  const cols = ["Who sends to whom, and when", "Amounts converted in or out", "Total held in confidential form", "A holder's balance", "A Send's amount"];
+  const rows: [string, [string, string][]][] = [
+    ["Anyone", [["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["no, unless it is a recognisable zero", PART], ["no", NO]]],
+    ["A holder", [["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["their own, and any recognisable zero", PART], ["their own only", PART]]],
+    ["The issuer", [["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["every one", YES], ["every one", YES]]],
+    ["An auditor, if the token names one", [["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["yes", YES], ["every one", YES], ["every one", YES]]],
+  ];
+  return (
+    <Figure caption="Who can read what. A dashed cell is a partial yes. A recognisable zero is a new balance, an inbox just merged, or a position clawed back.">
+      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
+        <table className="w-full text-[11px] leading-snug border-separate border-spacing-1">
+          <thead>
+            <tr>
+              <th className="text-left font-semibold align-bottom p-1">who</th>
+              {cols.map((c) => <th key={c} className="text-left font-semibold align-bottom p-1">{c}</th>)}
+            </tr>
+          </thead>
+          <tbody>
+            {rows.map(([who, cells]) => (
+              <tr key={who}>
+                <th className="text-left font-semibold align-top p-1">{who}</th>
+                {cells.map(([t, cls], i) => <td key={i} className={`rounded border p-1 align-top ${cls}`}>{t}</td>)}
+              </tr>
+            ))}
+          </tbody>
+        </table>
+      </div>
+    </Figure>
+  );
+}

+      </div>

+/** Observer and issuer pages: one position, field by field. */
+export function PositionAnatomyDiagram({ focus }: { focus: "observer" | "issuer" }) {
+  const ring = (on: boolean) => (on ? "ring-2 ring-offset-1 ring-violet-400 dark:ring-offset-black rounded-md" : "");
+  return (
+    <Figure caption={focus === "issuer"
+      ? "One confidential position on the ledger. The issuer's copy sits beside the holder's own, encrypted to the issuer's key; the next panels show that each copy is there, never the figure."
+      : "One confidential position on the ledger, and what an observer can tell from each field. The panels below read these fields for four test accounts."}>
+      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
+        <Node tone="public" title="Public balance" field="MPTAmount">an ordinary amount anyone can read</Node>
+        <Node tone="public" title="The holder's encryption key" field="HolderEncryptionKey">public once registered, and never changeable</Node>
+        <Node tone="hidden" title="Spending balance, encrypted to the holder" field="ConfidentialBalanceSpending">value hidden, unless it is the recognisable zero</Node>
+        <Node tone="hidden" title="Inbox, encrypted to the holder" field="ConfidentialBalanceInbox">value hidden, unless it is the recognisable zero</Node>
+        <div className={ring(focus === "issuer")}>
+          <Node tone="issuer" title="The issuer's copy, encrypted to the issuer" field="IssuerEncryptedBalance">kept beside every confidential balance; the issuer can read it</Node>
+        </div>
+        <Node tone="issuer" title="The auditor's copy, if the token names an auditor" field="AuditorEncryptedBalance">the auditor can read it</Node>
+        <Node tone="public" title="Version counter" field="ConfidentialBalanceVersion">counts changes to the balance, publicly</Node>

+
+/** Who can do what, and wallets: the two keys a confidential transaction needs. */
+export function TwoKeysDiagram({ wallet = false }: { wallet?: boolean }) {
+  return (
+    <Figure caption={wallet
+      ? "A confidential transaction needs both keys. The wallets examined supply only the account key, so a page cannot assemble one through them."
+      : "A confidential transaction needs both keys: the proof comes from the ElGamal key and the signature from the account key."}>
+      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto_1.4fr] items-center">
+        <Node title="The master seed" sub="what an XRPL wallet keeps" />
+        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
+          <Edge label="XRPL's own derivation gives the account key" />
+          <Edge label="this project's derivation, not a published standard, gives the ElGamal key" />
+        </div>
+        <div className="space-y-2">
+          <Node title="The account key" sub="signs every transaction, public or confidential">
+            {wallet && <span>A browser wallet holds this one.</span>}
+          </Node>
+          <Node tone="hidden" title="The ElGamal key" sub="reads the holder's balances; builds the proofs">
+            {wallet && <span>None of the wallets examined on 2026-08-28 keeps it or gives it to a page.</span>}
+          </Node>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+      <div className="mt-2">
+        <Node dashed title="Or a standalone ElGamal key, kept separately">Two secrets to keep instead of one.</Node>
+      </div>
+    </Figure>
+  );
+}

+      </div>
+      <Legend tones={["hidden"]} />
+    </Figure>
+  );
